# Unify profile typography across phone, tablet, and desktop

**Audit baseline: 13 October 2026.** The measurements and initial mapping below were recorded before implementation. The approved implementation now covers the header and Identity, Brain, Curation, Collected, xTDH, Subscriptions, and Proxy views.

The final review retained the existing responsive profile name, badges, post content, and Collected chart typography. Identity score numbers remain semibold and Rep remains blue. Header statistics retain Montserrat’s normal numerals instead of tabular numerals, following the final visual review. Empty-state copy uses subdued supporting text. Existing buttons remain unchanged except for the explicitly requested Rep category action placement/compact size and Proxy tab presentation. These approved exceptions take precedence over the initial proposal below. Browser implementation checks cover 390, 768, and 1280px; native screenshots below remain baseline evidence, not a post-change native verification.

[Mapping](#consolidated-audit-and-migration-map) · [Risks](#inconsistency-and-mobile-risk-register) · [Implementation](#implementation-recommendation-and-expected-footprint) · [Screenshots](#before-screenshot-index) · [Measured inventory](#detailed-measured-audit-and-role-mapping)

## Result and evidence boundary

The inconsistency is confirmed. Equivalent text roles vary in size, weight, leading, spacing and color; the biggest concentration is in Collected, Brain, subscription controls, and the compact Identity selectors. The proposed scale is a good basis, with three qualifications: preserve contrast on filled/semantic controls, retain 16px inputs on touch iOS at tablet widths, and treat the three compact Identity selector counts as UI text rather than full display numbers.

The locally collected evidence includes **336 grouped rendered styles/roles, 173 exact computed typography tuples, 916 source declarations across 220 files, and 74 browser screenshots**.

The detailed table below records computed browser typography and maps each observed element group to a target role. The source inventory covers conditional declarations that could not be rendered without changing data. **Source-only entries are not presented as measured screenshots.** This is an audit of current code and live read-only states, not a guarantee that every conditional branch has been exercised.

Baseline: isolated audit worktree at `f41b597ac4c759f5b8a0dcb2a1a3c02e49ce1f1f` (the locally recorded `origin/main`). The measurements are tied to this commit. Local Next.js development runtime; actual project dependencies installed through the repository wrapper. Public production API data was read through the local frontend.

Profiles inspected: a data-heavy public profile, a signed-in seeded QA account with a nearly empty profile, and a second public profile with populated Curation and Brain data. Live text, wallet addresses, account identifiers and screenshot binaries are omitted from this repository copy; the original local audit retains the evidence.

Browser evidence: 390×844, 768×1024 and 1280×1000 CSS-pixel viewports, full-page PNGs and `getComputedStyle` after font readiness. The 390 context includes touch/mobile emulation. The 768 layout still uses compact Identity selectors because that feature switches at **lg/1024**, not md/768. Color/weight/context variants within a role are retained in the table. Figures and timestamps are sampled from live data and can change.

Native evidence: **real-device-runtime**, physical iPhone 14 Pro Max, iOS 26.6.2, 430×932 logical screen / 1290×2796 screenshot. This supplements the 390px browser capture. A no-push test shell loaded the local frontend and was controlled through XCTest/WebDriverAgent. Page sources verified native app and profile content. Read-only tab navigation and sheet open/close checks succeeded. Native auth state was preserved; the browser used the dedicated QA login.

No ratings, grants, follows, proxy assignments, purchases, profile edits or Curation changes were submitted. Browser writes to the public API were intercepted and aborted; one cookie-consent DELETE was intercepted before reaching the API. Authentication/session setup was the only authorized account setup. Native interactions were navigation, searching a public profile, and opening/closing sheets.

### Limits that affect interpretation

- Browser image requests intermittently failed, leaving some avatar/art placeholders. The screenshots preserve this actual local state; no replacement artwork was inserted. Typography is measured independently of image loading.
- The primary public profile’s Brain activity reached an error state; a direct read returned HTTP 304 without a body. The error is captured, and another populated profile was checked. Loading/error/empty typography remains in scope.
- Only actual read-only data was used. Populated proxy permissions, some grant configuration/results and conditional subscription history states are source-audited, not fabricated or submitted. In particular, `ProxyCreate.onTargetSelect` immediately calls the create mutation for a new recipient; stopping before selection is necessary to obey the no-actions instruction.
- Native subscriptions are omitted in this non-US iOS environment, and the direct destination returned to Identity. No regional gate was bypassed. Web subscriptions are captured at all three widths. Native owned proxy/setup states were not exercised with the user's account.
- Browser computed values are exact CSS evidence; native screenshots/page sources establish the native rendering and interactions, not separately extracted native computed CSS. No Dynamic Type, alternate locale, VoiceOver, 200% zoom or Android audit was performed.
- Source inventory includes conditional/orphan candidates under the explicitly requested `components/user/**` tree. Do not blindly restyle a declaration merely because it is listed: confirm its role and active route consumer during implementation.

## Verification of the initial observations

| Observation                                | Finding at the audited commit                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| About nine uppercase variants              | Confirmed and understated when line height and shared components are included. The measured dictionary contains numerous distinct uppercase tuples, including 9/10/11/12px labels and a 14px NIC status; normal/wide/wider/widest and custom 0.06em tracking occur. These are separated into Labels, Badges, status UI and tabs in the mapping.                                 |
| Sixteen one-off sizes between 7.5 and 15px | Not reproduced exactly in `components/user` TSX. There are **12 literal spellings at or below 15px, representing 10 numeric sizes**: 7.5, 8, 8.5, 9, 10, 10.4, 11, 12.8, 13 and 15px. The aliases are `0.625rem`=10px and `0.6875rem`=11px. An additional `1rem` spelling is 16px. Shared rendered content also contributes inherited sizes, including 13⅓px from HTML `small`. |
| Five weights, including light and bold     | Confirmed: 300, 400, 500, 600, 700. Subscription explanations explicitly use 300. Brain titles, collection eyebrows, level digits and Beta use 700.                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Existing semantic tokens barely used       | `tw-text-label`, `tw-text-meta`, `tw-text-body`: **zero literal uses in `components/user` TSX**. `tw-text-md`: **16 literal occurrences**. These counts exclude dynamically assembled classes and other directories.                                                                                                                                                            |
| Equivalent roles differ                    | Confirmed: section/card titles 14/16/18/20/24; major figures 18/20/24/30; time/raters/notes commonly 10/11/12/13/14; uppercase labels span several leading/tracking/color combinations. Detailed variants are below.                                                                                                                                                            |

[tailwind.config.ts](../../../tailwind.config.ts) currently defines `label` as 11px with **0.14em tracking and no explicit line height**; `meta` as 13px without leading; `body` as 15px with **1.72 leading = 25.8px**; `md` as 15/24; `xxs` as 13/20. Thus simply replacing arbitrary classes with the existing names would **not** implement the requested scale. Target `wider` is the existing Tailwind 0.05em, not 0.14em.

## Target scale and interpretation

All numbers below are CSS pixels. Tight tracking = −0.025em; normal = 0; wider = 0.05em. All figures use `tabular-nums`, including balances, scores, counts, IDs, percentages and dates. Inline numeric text inherits its surrounding role's size and weight.

| Target role           |                                             390 | ≥768 | Weight | Leading 390 / ≥768 | Tracking | Color                                                     |
| --------------------- | ----------------------------------------------: | ---: | -----: | ------------------ | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| Profile name          |                                              20 |   24 |    600 | 28 / 32            | tight    | iron-50                                                   |
| Section title         |                                              18 |   20 |    600 | 24 / 28            | tight    | iron-100                                                  |
| Card title            |                                              15 |   15 |    600 | 20                 | normal   | iron-100                                                  |
| Body                  |                                              15 |   15 |    400 | 24                 | normal   | iron-300                                                  |
| UI text               |                                              14 |   14 |    500 | 20                 | normal   | iron-100; secondary iron-300                              |
| Main tabs             |                                              15 |   16 |    500 | 20                 | normal   | iron-400; active iron-50                                  |
| Controls              |                                              13 |   13 |    500 | 18                 | normal   | iron-500; active iron-100, with contrast exceptions below |
| Meta                  |                                              13 |   13 |    400 | 18                 | normal   | iron-500                                                  |
| Label                 |                                              11 |   11 |    500 | 16                 | wider    | iron-500                                                  |
| Display number        |                                              28 |   30 |    500 | 32 / 36            | tight    | iron-50                                                   |
| Unit                  |                                              14 |   14 |    400 | 20                 | normal   | iron-500                                                  |
| Badge / pill          |                                              10 |   10 |    600 | 14                 | wider    | per badge                                                 |
| Form inputs           |                                              16 |   14 |    400 | 20                 | normal   | iron-100; retain 16 on touch iOS                          |
| Level badge exception | Existing 8.5 digits only inside the 20px circle | same |    600 | preserve centering | normal   | per level                                                 |

The level exception is limited to the **20px** circle. The current 18px compact circle with 7.5px digits is not covered by it: use a 20px circle with the permitted digits, or a 10px Badge if that consumer cannot use the circle. Do not preserve its 700 weight.

### Places the scale needs qualification

1. **Control color depends on its surface.** Exact sRGB contrast from the repository palette: `iron-500` on black = 5.68:1, on iron-950 = 5.02:1, on iron-900 = 4.59:1, but on **iron-800 = 4.04:1**. The latter misses the repository's 4.5:1 normal-text standard. It is only 3.22:1 on iron-100. Preserve the dark inverse foreground of the light Follow button and use iron-300 or iron-100 for readable text on lighter dark fills. Success/error/NIC state colors carry meaning and should remain semantic while adopting the relevant size/weight/leading. This needs no new size.
2. **A 768px breakpoint does not identify a mouse device.** The current native rule in [styles/globals.css](../../../styles/globals.css) protects input, textarea, select and editable content with at least 16px; rating inputs already opt into their own larger typography. Keep that safeguard at tablet widths. Treat 14px as the desktop/fine-pointer input variant. This is a recommendation based on existing code and the requested iOS zoom requirement, not a claim of a measured iPad focus test.
3. **Compact selector figures are controls, not display scores.** The three Identity selector cards already truncate the long Rep total at 390px. Substituting 28px into all three would worsen clipping. Map their figures to UI text 14/20/500 + tabular numbers, and reserve Display number for the prominent score within a detail view. If all three selectors must retain large scores, a layout change is required and should be approved separately; no new font size is necessary.
4. **Heatmap/season quantities are meta.** Promote 8–9px dates/progress to 13px Meta, even though this requires more space, fewer month labels or a larger cell/footer track. They are useful information rather than decorative badge text. Use 10px Badge only for compact state markers such as eligibility and Primary.
5. **Rich content must retain document semantics.** Curation/Brain user-authored paragraphs map to Body, inline links inherit Body, headings use Card/Section title by hierarchy, `strong` uses 600 and `em` may stay italic. Do not flatten article headings or external embed layouts with an unrestricted descendant selector. Embedded third-party content and the text baked into artwork are not app typography tokens.

## Inconsistency and mobile-risk register

1. **Under10px outside the permitted exception.** Heatmap month/day9px; Collected season progress9px and not-started label/buttons 8px; subscription eligibility9px; desktop Primary9px. The 7.5px digits in an18px level circle are also outside the stated exception. All locations are listed above and in the source inventory. The observed8.5px digits in a20px circle are the sole accepted size exception, but still need600 weight.
2. **Body is too small on mobile.** Bio14px, Rep/empty-state explanations14px, activity sentences14px, subscription explanations14px, and some shared Brain content14px. Use Body15/24 across all widths rather than sm-only fixes.
3. **Meta is frequently below 13px.** Rep raters 12px, activity times12px, NFT ID/quantity/rank11px, season/progress8–10px, Brain post ages12px, subscription schedule dates12px, edited marker10px. These should not be relabeled as Badges merely to keep them small.
4. **Mobile editable fields.** Confirmed14px: xTDH NFT contract search (`NftPickerSearch`) and subscription custom card count (`UserPageSubscriptionsSection`). The bio sheet is16px at 390, so it is not a phone-sheet failure; its inline768px editor is14px. Rep search16px and Rep/NIC amount18px are safe on size. Proxy search16px and name editor16px are also already large enough. Received-xTDH collection search is16px at 390 and 14px at 768/1280. Keep native protection; test Safari focus separately during implementation.
5. **Weights drift.** Remove light300 and bold700. Current600 vs500 variants are also inconsistent within controls, table values and metadata. Adopting the target role must include weight rather than just changing `text-*`.
6. **Leading is inconsistent and often missing.** Profile name20/20 on phone; totals30/30; category title14/20; NFT title14/19.25; descriptions14/22.75; multiple arbitrary sizes inherit `normal`. Set an explicit line height for every semantic role.
7. **Tracking does not follow meaning.** Brain14px titles use wide; season headers tight; labels mix normal, wide, wider, widest and 0.06em; existing label token uses0.14em. Normalize Labels to wider, titles/display to tight, Body/UI/Meta to normal.
8. **Color hierarchy is inconsistent.** Body alternates iron-300/400/500; timestamps can be iron-600; similar numbers are white, iron-50/100/200/300 or primary. Iron-600 has only 3.39:1 contrast on black and 2.99:1 on iron-950, insufficient for normal small text. Promote essential dates/empty messages and follow the surface-aware exception above.
9. **Responsive typography changes at several boundaries.** Bio and coverage body change at sm640; Brain sidebar labels differ between mobile-strip and desktop renderers; Primary shrinks at lg1024; compact Identity persists at 768; name changes at md768. Layout can keep appropriate breakpoints, but typography should obey the approved role's md-only rule.
10. **Numeric alignment is incomplete.** xTDH metrics and rating helpers explicitly use tabular numbers, but header stats, many Rep totals, collection IDs, season counts, subscription values and timestamps do not. Apply tabular figures consistently without increasing unrelated letter spacing.
11. **Truncation in the390 Identity selectors.** The long Rep total is already visually ellipsized. Larger target display text would amplify it; use the compact-count mapping above.
12. **Proxy entry widens the mobile layout.** At a requested390px mobile viewport, opening Assign Proxy produced a417px layout viewport. `ProxyCreateTargetSearch` has a fixed `tw-w-80` input inside the narrower profile area. This can make the entire page appear smaller even though the input computes to 16px. Fix width containment with the typography change, and verify both layout and visual viewport sizes; a simple `scrollWidth > innerWidth` check missed this case.
13. **Small interactive text targets.** Measured 390 examples: Rep rater buttons 36–53×16px; category title buttons 126–187×20px; follower name links about 15px high; Collected activity links 18px high; several profile/inline links 20px high. These need a target/spacing review and usually a larger row/button hit area, not inflated font sizes. Some inline links can qualify for WCAG exceptions; measurements alone do not establish a blanket failure. Prefer 44px touch comfort for standalone actions while retaining the repository's minimum-size/spacing rules.
14. **Native horizontal tab navigation needs care.** xTDH required scrolling the profile tab strip; a partially obscured Identity link could hit the scroll control instead. Subsequent visible scroll-left and Identity taps succeeded. Preserve visible focus and adequate hit areas when adjusting tab leading and padding.
15. **Compact tables need layout follow-through.** Collected details contain wide numeric tables and mobile disclosures. Increasing Meta/Label sizes must preserve intentional internal scrolling, minimum content width and accessible table labels; do not compensate by shrinking text or hiding data.

## Implementation recommendation and expected footprint

Use semantic Tailwind font-size tokens with explicit leading/tracking, plus a small shared role-to-class preset for weight/color and responsive pairs. Prefer a profile-scoped prefix initially (for example `tw-text-profile-body`, `tw-text-profile-meta`) because changing today's global `body`/`label` token definitions would alter unrelated consumers. Do not introduce new numeric sizes beyond the approved scale.

- Put size, leading and tracking in [tailwind.config.ts](../../../tailwind.config.ts). Keep colors and contextual states in the role preset/consumer. Keep responsive names literal so Tailwind can discover them.
- Use one static preset per semantic role, with md variants only for name, section titles, tabs, display numbers and desktop inputs. Components should select the role once rather than repeat several arbitrary utility values.
- Pass scoped typography variants to shared Button, tabs/dropdowns, pagination, dialogs, activity rows, NFT search, wave/drop rendering and followers. Avoid globally changing their other-route consumers in this profile-only change.
- Keep Body inheritance for inline prose and its links. Set semibold for semantic emphasis. Keep actual semantic headings and labels in JSX; a typography preset is not a replacement for document structure.
- Add tabular numbers at figure-bearing wrappers/rows and remove conflicting weight/leading/tracking classes at the same time. Preserve the native input rule.
- Do not add a general-purpose Typography React component or a package just for this migration. A small static class map plus existing `cn`/class composition is enough.

**Expected implementation size: approximately 215 files total**, with a planning range of 195–225. The source candidate inventory currently spans 220 typography-bearing files (175 under `components/user` plus 45 observed shared consumers); not every container/color declaration will require editing. Allow 1 config/preset boundary and 4–8 focused test/evidence updates. This is a forecast grounded in the inventory, not a promise that every listed file will change. The measured roles and source links make the final edit set reviewable before coding.

Suggested implementation sequence: tokens/presets and shared opt-ins; header/tabs/dialogs; Identity; Brain/Curation; Collected; xTDH/Subscriptions/proxy; then responsive and native regression review. Keep behavior, data requests, action semantics and regional purchasing gates unchanged.

Validation for the subsequent implementation:

- Review/update the relevant portions of `tests/social/waves-profile-readonly.spec.ts` and `tests/social/profile-deep-links-readonly.spec.ts`, and preserve pack selection through `tests/packs.manifest.cjs`. Do not weaken behavioral expectations to accommodate styling.
- Add a small computed-style/browser contract for representative roles at 390,768,1280, including an iOS-size input guard and the417px proxy case. Avoid hundreds of tests mirroring individual utility strings.
- Verify long handles, seven/eight-digit scores, long category names, dense tables, empty states, selected/unselected controls and modal headings with the existing public/QA profiles.
- Recheck Safari/native focus, touch targets, portrait tab navigation, contrast and non-US iOS subscription omission. Run the authorized repository changed-file lint/typecheck/react-doctor checks after implementation; this report contains no application change requiring them now.

## Current computed-style dictionary

All dimensions are CSS pixels. `normal` is the actual computed CSS keyword, not an assumed pixel value. Tracking is the computed letter spacing; Tailwind’s em-based spacing therefore appears as pixels. All observed app text resolves to Montserrat/sans-serif except explicit monospace address/code elements. Colors are exact configured token matches or literal computed values.

## Source inventory for conditional and shared states

This inventory supplements runtime evidence. It includes every JSX typography declaration in `components/user/**` and the shared components observed above; it also catches conditionals, loading/errors, edit states and proxy configurations that were not safely reachable in the read-only account. It is not a claim that every branch was rendered. The classes show responsive prefixes and inheritance explicitly. Blank size/weight/color in a declaration means inherited; use the measured role table where available. Constants and global overrides are discussed in the findings.
